[debug]     
[debug]     SGLang Diffusion Model Serving
[debug]     
[debug]     options:
[debug]       -h, --help            show this help message and exit
[debug]       --config CONFIG       Read CLI options from a config JSON or YAML file.
[debug]       --model-path MODEL_PATH
[debug]                             The path of the model weights. This can be a local
[debug]                             folder or a Hugging Face repo ID.
[debug]       --model-id MODEL_ID   Override the model ID used for config resolution.
[debug]                             Useful when --model-path is a local directory whose
[debug]                             name does not match any registered HF repo name.
[debug]                             Should be the repo name portion of the HF ID (e.g.
[debug]                             'Qwen-Image' for 'Qwen/Qwen-Image').
[debug]       --pipeline-class-name PIPELINE_CLASS_NAME
[debug]                             Override pipeline class selection from
[debug]                             model_index.json. Must match a registered
[debug]                             pipeline_name.
[debug]       --attention-backend ATTENTION_BACKEND
[debug]                             The attention backend to use. For SGLang-native
[debug]                             pipelines, use values like fa, torch_sdpa, sage_attn,
[debug]                             etc. For diffusers pipelines, use diffusers attention
[debug]                             backend names such as flash, _flash_3_hub, sage, or
[debug]                             xformers.
[debug]       --attention-backend-config ATTENTION_BACKEND_CONFIG
[debug]                             Configuration for the attention backend. Can be a JSON
[debug]                             string, a path to a JSON/YAML file, or key=value
[debug]                             pairs.
[debug]       --component-attention-backends COMPONENT_ATTENTION_BACKENDS
[debug]                             Per-component attention backend overrides for native
[debug]                             pipelines. Use component names from model_index.json,
[debug]                             e.g. 'text_encoder=torch_sdpa,transformer=fa'.
[debug]       --cache-dit-config CACHE_DIT_CONFIG
[debug]                             Path to a Cache-DiT YAML/JSON config. Enables cache-
[debug]                             dit for diffusers backend.
[debug]       --trust-remote-code [TRUST_REMOTE_CODE]
[debug]                             Trust remote code when loading HuggingFace models
[debug]       --revision REVISION   The specific model version to use (can be a branch
[debug]                             name, tag name, or commit id)
[debug]       --performance-mode {manual,auto,speed,memory}, --mode {manual,auto,speed,memory}
[debug]                             Preset for performance and memory defaults. 'manual'
[debug]                             keeps performance-related server args under explicit
[debug]                             user control, no adjustment is made; 'auto' keeps safe
[debug]                             defaults and applies high-confidence FSDP/CFG
[debug]                             improvements; 'speed' favors GPU-resident execution
[debug]                             for lower latency and higher throughput, and may OOM;
[debug]                             'memory' favors lower GPU memory usage; Explicit
[debug]                             offload/FSDP/parallelism flags take precedence.
[debug]       --num-gpus NUM_GPUS   The number of GPUs to use.
[debug]       --base-gpu-id BASE_GPU_ID
[debug]                             The starting GPU ID for this instance. Used with
[debug]                             --disagg-role to place role instances on specific GPUs
[debug]                             without CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES.
[debug]       --gpu-ids GPU_IDS [GPU_IDS ...]
[debug]                             Physical GPU IDs for this instance, e.g. --gpu-ids 0 1
[debug]                             6 7 or --gpu-ids 0,1,6,7. Overrides --base-gpu-id for
[debug]                             standalone disagg roles.
[debug]       --tp-size TP_SIZE     The tensor parallelism size. Defaults to 1 if not
[debug]                             specified.
[debug]       --sp-degree SP_DEGREE
[debug]                             The sequence parallelism size. If not specified, will
[debug]                             use all remaining GPUs after accounting for TP and DP.
[debug]       --ulysses-degree ULYSSES_DEGREE
[debug]                             Ulysses sequence parallel degree. Used in attention
[debug]                             layer.
[debug]       --ring-degree RING_DEGREE
[debug]                             Ring sequence parallel degree. Used in attention
[debug]                             layer.
[debug]       --enable-cfg-parallel [ENABLE_CFG_PARALLEL]
[debug]                             Enable cfg parallel at degree 2. Auto-enabled when
[debug]                             num_gpus >= 2 and no SP flags are set. Use false to
[debug]                             disable it explicitly.
[debug]       --cfg-parallel-size CFG_PARALLEL_DEGREE
[debug]                             Number of GPUs per CFG parallel group (1 = disabled, N
[debug]                             > 1 = enabled at degree N). Supersedes --enable-cfg-
[debug]                             parallel. Allows 4-branch CFG parallel (e.g., --cfg-
[debug]                             parallel-size 4) for models with cond + neg +
[debug]                             perturbed + modality branches.
[debug]       --data-parallel-size DP_SIZE, --dp-size DP_SIZE, --dp DP_SIZE
[debug]                             The data parallelism size.
[debug]       --hsdp-replicate-dim HSDP_REPLICATE_DIM
[debug]                             The data parallelism size.
[debug]       --hsdp-shard-dim HSDP_SHARD_DIM
[debug]                             The data parallelism shards. Defaults to num_gpus if
[debug]                             not specified.
[debug]       --dist-timeout DIST_TIMEOUT
[debug]                             Timeout for torch.distributed operations in seconds.
[debug]                             Increase this value if you encounter 'Connection
[debug]                             closed by peer' errors after the service is idle.
[debug]       --disagg-role {monolithic,encoder,denoiser,decoder,server,denoising}
[debug]                             Role for disaggregated pipeline.
[debug]       --disagg-timeout DISAGG_TIMEOUT
[debug]                             Timeout in seconds for pending disagg requests.
[debug]                             Default: 3600.
[debug]       --disagg-downstream-wait-timeout DISAGG_DOWNSTREAM_WAIT_TIMEOUT
[debug]                             Timeout in seconds while waiting for a downstream role
[debug]                             slot. Default: 1800.
[debug]       --disagg-dispatch-policy {round_robin,max_free_slots}
[debug]                             Dispatch policy for pool mode disagg routing.
[debug]       --disagg-instance-id DISAGG_INSTANCE_ID
[debug]                             Stable per-role instance ID used by DiffusionServer
[debug]                             registration.
[debug]       --disagg-max-slots-per-instance DISAGG_MAX_SLOTS_PER_INSTANCE
[debug]                             Maximum concurrent transfer/computation slots tracked
[debug]                             per instance.
[debug]       --disagg-transfer-redundancy DISAGG_TRANSFER_REDUNDANCY
[debug]                             Redundancy factor used when sizing transfer buffers
[debug]                             from warmup.
[debug]       --disagg-role-device {auto,cpu,cuda}
[debug]                             Per-role device override. 'cpu' is intended for same-
[debug]                             machine encoder roles.
[debug]       --disagg-transfer-backend {auto,mock,mooncake}
[debug]                             Transfer backend for multimodal diffusion
[debug]                             disaggregation.
[debug]       --disagg-transfer-pool-size DISAGG_TRANSFER_POOL_SIZE
[debug]                             Size of the P2P transfer buffer pool in bytes.
[debug]       --disagg-transfer-pin-memory {auto,off,required}
[debug]                             CUDA host-register same-host shared-memory transfer
[debug]                             buffers.
[debug]       --disagg-p2p-hostname DISAGG_P2P_HOSTNAME
[debug]                             Hostname for P2P transfer engine.
[debug]       --disagg-ib-device DISAGG_IB_DEVICE
[debug]                             InfiniBand device for P2P RDMA transfers.
[debug]       --disagg-server-addr DISAGG_SERVER_ADDR
[debug]                             DiffusionServer head node address for per-role launch
[debug]                             mode.
[debug]       --encoder-urls ENCODER_URLS
[debug]                             Encoder instance work endpoints for DiffusionServer
[debug]                             head mode.
[debug]       --denoiser-urls DENOISER_URLS
[debug]                             Denoiser instance work endpoints for DiffusionServer
[debug]                             head mode.
[debug]       --decoder-urls DECODER_URLS
[debug]                             Decoder instance work endpoints for DiffusionServer
[debug]                             head mode.
[debug]       --encoder-tp ENCODER_TP
[debug]                             Tensor parallelism for encoder role.
[debug]       --denoiser-tp DENOISER_TP
[debug]                             Tensor parallelism for denoiser role.
[debug]       --denoiser-sp DENOISER_SP
[debug]                             Sequence parallelism for denoiser role.
[debug]       --denoiser-ulysses DENOISER_ULYSSES
[debug]                             Ulysses SP degree for denoiser role.
[debug]       --denoiser-ring DENOISER_RING
[debug]                             Ring SP degree for denoiser role.
[debug]       --decoder-sp DECODER_SP
[debug]                             Sequence parallelism for decoder role.
[debug]       --decoder-tp DECODER_TP
[debug]                             Deprecated alias for --decoder-sp.
[debug]       --prompt-file-path PROMPT_FILE_PATH
[debug]                             Path to a text file containing prompts (one per line)
[debug]                             for batch processing
[debug]       --mask-strategy-file-path MASK_STRATEGY_FILE_PATH
[debug]                             Path to mask strategy JSON file for STA
[debug]       --enable-torch-compile [ENABLE_TORCH_COMPILE]
[debug]                             Use torch.compile to speed up DiT inference.However,
[debug]                             will likely cause precision drifts. See
[debug]                             (https://github.com/pytorch/pytorch/issues/145213)
[debug]       --enable-layerwise-nvtx-marker [ENABLE_LAYERWISE_NVTX_MARKER]
[debug]                             Enable layerwise NVTX markers for profiling with
[debug]                             Nsight Systems. Adds NVTX ranges around each pipeline
[debug]                             stage, the denoising loop, every denoising step, the
[debug]                             predict_noise / scheduler_step sub-operations, and
[debug]                             every transformer submodule forward (recursive).
[debug]                             Warmup steps are excluded to keep captured traces
[debug]                             clean.
[debug]       --warmup-mode {off,request,server}
[debug]                             Warmup mode (canonical knob). One of: `off` (no
[debug]                             warmup); `request` (request-based: warm on real
[debug]                             incoming requests); `server` (server-based: a
[debug]                             synthetic warmup request right after the server is
[debug]                             ready, before traffic). Takes precedence over the
[debug]                             deprecated --warmup/--server-warmup. `sglang serve`
[debug]                             defaults to `server`; other entrypoints default to
[debug]                             request-based when warmup is enabled. When enabled,
[debug]                             look for the line ending with `(with warmup excluded)`
[debug]                             for actual processing time.
[debug]       --warmup [WARMUP]     [DEPRECATED: use --warmup-mode] Perform warmup before
[debug]                             normal traffic. Maps to --warmup-mode request (or
[debug]                             server, combined with --server-warmup). Recommended
[debug]                             when benchmarking for fair comparison and best
[debug]                             performance.
[debug]       --warmup-resolutions WARMUP_RESOLUTIONS [WARMUP_RESOLUTIONS ...]
[debug]                             Specify explicit warmup resolutions. e.g., `--warmup-
[debug]                             resolutions 256x256 720x720`
[debug]       --warmup-steps WARMUP_STEPS
[debug]                             The number of warmup steps to perform for each
[debug]                             resolution.
[debug]       --server-warmup [SERVER_WARMUP]
[debug]                             [DEPRECATED: use --warmup-mode server] Send a
[debug]                             synthetic warmup request after the server is ready
[debug]                             (server-based warmup).
[debug]       --dit-cpu-offload [DIT_CPU_OFFLOAD]
[debug]                             Use CPU offload for DiT inference. Enable if run out
[debug]                             of memory with FSDP.
[debug]       --dit-layerwise-offload [DIT_LAYERWISE_OFFLOAD]
[debug]                             Enable layerwise CPU offload with async H2D prefetch
[debug]                             overlap for DiTs. It selects only the DiT layerwise
[debug]                             group. Cannot be used together with cache-dit
[debug]                             (SGLANG_CACHE_DIT_ENABLED) or use_fsdp_inference. May
[debug]                             be combined with --dit-cpu-offload, in which case DiT
[debug]                             weights stay on host memory and only the layers needed
[debug]                             for the current step are brought on-device (lowest
[debug]                             peak GPU memory).
[debug]       --layerwise-offload-components LAYERWISE_OFFLOAD_COMPONENTS [LAYERWISE_OFFLOAD_COMPONENTS ...], --layerwise-offload-modules LAYERWISE_OFFLOAD_COMPONENTS [LAYERWISE_OFFLOAD_COMPONENTS ...]
[debug]                             Select pipeline components for layerwise offload. Use
[debug]                             dit to select the DiT layerwise group, default for the
[debug]                             default group (currently text_encoder, image_encoder,
[debug]                             and vae), or all to select every layerwise-offloadable
[debug]                             component. This option does not imply --dit-layerwise-
[debug]                             offload. Example: --layerwise-offload-components
[debug]                             text_encoder image_encoder vae.
[debug]       --dit-offload-prefetch-size DIT_OFFLOAD_PREFETCH_SIZE
[debug]                             The size of prefetch for dit-layerwise-offload. If the
[debug]                             value is between 0.0 and 1.0, it is treated as a ratio
[debug]                             of the total number of layers. If the value is >= 1,
[debug]                             it is treated as the absolute number of layers. 0.0
[debug]                             means prefetch 1 layer (lowest memory). Values above
[debug]                             0.5 might have peak memory close to no offload but
[debug]                             worse performance.
[debug]       --text-encoder-cpu-offload [TEXT_ENCODER_CPU_OFFLOAD]
[debug]                             Use CPU offload for text encoder. Enable if run out of
[debug]                             memory.
[debug]       --image-encoder-cpu-offload [IMAGE_ENCODER_CPU_OFFLOAD]
[debug]                             Use CPU offload for image encoder. Enable if run out
[debug]                             of memory.
[debug]       --vae-cpu-offload [VAE_CPU_OFFLOAD]
[debug]                             Use CPU offload for VAE. Enable if run out of memory.
[debug]       --use-fsdp-inference [USE_FSDP_INFERENCE]
[debug]                             Use FSDP inference to shard DiT weights across GPUs.
[debug]                             For single-GPU memory pressure, prefer CPU or
[debug]                             layerwise offload.
[debug]       --pin-cpu-memory [PIN_CPU_MEMORY]
[debug]                             Pin memory for CPU offload. Only added as a temp
[debug]                             workaround if it throws "CUDA error: invalid
[debug]                             argument". Should be enabled in almost all cases
[debug]       --ltx2-two-stage-device-mode {original,resident,snapshot}
[debug]                             LTX-2.3 two-stage device residency mode: 'original'
[debug]                             keeps official two-stage semantics without premerged
[debug]                             stage2, 'resident' keeps both transformers resident on
[debug]                             GPU. 'snapshot' is deprecated, treated as 'original',
[debug]                             and may be removed after two release cycles. Default
[debug]                             is auto: resident on H200/high-memory CUDA GPUs,
[debug]                             otherwise original.
[debug]       --disable-autocast [DISABLE_AUTOCAST]
[debug]                             Disable autocast for denoising loop and vae decoding
[debug]                             in pipeline sampling
[debug]       --quantization QUANTIZATION
[debug]                             Quantization method for the transformer. If omitted,
[debug]                             the method is auto-detected from the checkpoint config
[debug]                             or safetensors metadata when possible. Use this flag
[debug]                             to override auto-detection. Online (post-load)
[debug]                             quantization from a BF16/FP16 checkpoint is supported
[debug]                             for 'fp8' and 'mxfp4'. Other methods ('modelopt',
[debug]                             'modelopt_fp8', 'modelopt_fp4', 'mxfp8', 'mxfp4_npu',
[debug]                             'modelslim') require a pre-quantized checkpoint. Note:
[debug]                             'mxfp4' targets ROCm + MI350+ (gfx95x); 'mxfp4_npu' /
[debug]                             'mxfp8' target Ascend NPU (A5 series for mxfp4_npu).
[debug]       --quantization-ignored-layers QUANTIZATION_IGNORED_LAYERS [QUANTIZATION_IGNORED_LAYERS ...]
[debug]                             Layer name patterns to keep unquantized during online
[debug]                             quantization (fp8/mxfp4). Each pattern is matched
[debug]                             against the layer prefix. Example: --quantization-
[debug]                             ignored-layers img_mod txt_mod to_out
[debug]       --enable-svdquant [ENABLE_SVDQUANT]
[debug]                             Enable Nunchaku SVDQuant (W4A4-style) inference.
[debug]       --transformer-weights-path TRANSFORMER_WEIGHTS_PATH
[debug]                             Path to pre-quantized transformer weights. Can be a
[debug]                             single .safetensors file, a directory, or a
[debug]                             HuggingFace repo ID. Used by Nunchaku (SVDQuant) and
[debug]                             quantized single-file checkpoints.
[debug]       --quantization-precision QUANTIZATION_PRECISION
[debug]                             Quantization precision: int4 or nvfp4. If not
[debug]                             specified, inferred from model path or defaults to
[debug]                             int4.
[debug]       --quantization-rank QUANTIZATION_RANK
[debug]                             SVD low-rank dimension (e.g., 32). If not specified,
[debug]                             inferred from model path or defaults to 32.
[debug]       --quantization-act-unsigned [QUANTIZATION_ACT_UNSIGNED]
[debug]                             Use unsigned activation quantization (if supported).
[debug]       --master-port MASTER_PORT
[debug]                             Master port for distributed inference. If not set, a
[debug]                             random free port will be used.
[debug]       --scheduler-port SCHEDULER_PORT
[debug]                             Port for the scheduler server.
[debug]       --batching-mode {dynamic}
[debug]                             Request batching scheduler mode. Currently only
[debug]                             'dynamic' is implemented.
[debug]       --batching-max-size BATCHING_MAX_SIZE
[debug]                             Maximum number of compatible generation requests to
[debug]                             merge into one batch.
[debug]       --batching-delay-ms BATCHING_DELAY_MS
[debug]                             Maximum time (in ms) to wait for forming a larger
[debug]                             batch before dispatch.
[debug]       --batching-config BATCHING_CONFIG
[debug]                             Optional JSON file with {'schema_version': 1, 'rules':
[debug]                             [...]} batching admission rules that can cap
[debug]                             model/resolution shapes below --batching-max-size.

[debug]     
[debug]     options:
[debug]       -h, --help            show this help message and exit
[debug]       --model-path MODEL_PATH, --model MODEL_PATH
[debug]                             The path of the model weights. This can be a local
[debug]                             folder or a Hugging Face repo ID.
[debug]       --tokenizer-path TOKENIZER_PATH
[debug]                             The path of the tokenizer.
[debug]       --tokenizer-mode {auto,slow}
[debug]                             Tokenizer mode. 'auto' will use the fast tokenizer if
[debug]                             available, and 'slow' will always use the slow
[debug]                             tokenizer.
[debug]       --tokenizer-backend {huggingface,fastokens}
[debug]                             Tokenizer backend. 'huggingface' uses the default
[debug]                             HuggingFace tokenizers library, and 'fastokens' uses
[debug]                             the fastokens library for faster tokenization.
[debug]                             Requires the fastokens package to be installed.
[debug]       --tokenizer-worker-num TOKENIZER_WORKER_NUM
[debug]                             The worker num of the tokenizer manager.
[debug]       --detokenizer-worker-num DETOKENIZER_WORKER_NUM
[debug]                             The worker num of the detokenizer manager.
[debug]       --skip-tokenizer-init
[debug]                             If set, skip init tokenizer and pass input_ids in
[debug]                             generate request.
[debug]       --load-format {auto,pt,safetensors,npcache,dummy,sharded_state,gguf,bitsandbytes,mistral,layered,flash_rl,remote,remote_instance,fastsafetensors,private,runai_streamer}
[debug]                             The format of the model weights to load. "auto" will
[debug]                             try to load the weights in the safetensors format and
[debug]                             fall back to the pytorch bin format if safetensors
[debug]                             format is not available. "pt" will load the weights in
[debug]                             the pytorch bin format. "safetensors" will load the
[debug]                             weights in the safetensors format. "npcache" will load
[debug]                             the weights in pytorch format and store a numpy cache
[debug]                             to speed up the loading. "dummy" will initialize the
[debug]                             weights with random values, which is mainly for
[debug]                             profiling."gguf" will load the weights in the gguf
[debug]                             format. "bitsandbytes" will load the weights using
[debug]                             bitsandbytes quantization."layered" loads weights
[debug]                             layer by layer so that one can quantize a layer before
[debug]                             loading another to make the peak memory envelope
[debug]                             smaller.
[debug]       --model-loader-extra-config MODEL_LOADER_EXTRA_CONFIG
[debug]                             Extra config for model loader. This will be passed to
[debug]                             the model loader corresponding to the chosen
[debug]                             load_format.
[debug]       --trust-remote-code   Whether or not to allow for custom models defined on
[debug]                             the Hub in their own modeling files.
[debug]       --context-length CONTEXT_LENGTH
[debug]                             The model's maximum context length. Defaults to None
[debug]                             (will use the value from the model's config.json
[debug]                             instead). Supports standard SI suffixes (k, M, G, T)
[debug]                             and IEC suffixes (Ki, Mi, Gi, Ti). Suffixes are case-
[debug]                             sensitive. Decimals are allowed for SI suffixes only.
[debug]                             Examples: '1k' -> 1000 '1M' -> 1000000 '25.6k' ->
[debug]                             25600 '1Ki' -> 1024 '1Mi' -> 1048576
[debug]       --is-embedding        Whether to use a CausalLM as an embedding model.
[debug]       --enable-multimodal   Enable the multimodal functionality for the served
[debug]                             model. If the model being served is not multimodal,
[debug]                             nothing will happen
[debug]       --revision REVISION   The specific model version to use. It can be a branch
[debug]                             name, a tag name, or a commit id. If unspecified, will
[debug]                             use the default version.
[debug]       --model-impl MODEL_IMPL
[debug]                             Which implementation of the model to use. * "auto"
[debug]                             will try to use the SGLang implementation if it exists
[debug]                             and fall back to the Transformers implementation if no
[debug]                             SGLang implementation is available. * "sglang" will
[debug]                             use the SGLang model implementation. * "transformers"
[debug]                             will use the Transformers model * "mindspore" will use
[debug]                             the MindSpore model implementation.
[debug]       --model-config-parser MODEL_CONFIG_PARSER
[debug]                             Which model-config parser to use. "auto" picks
[debug]                             "mistral" via the is_mistral_model name heuristic,
[debug]                             else "hf" (AutoConfig over config.json). Plugins can
[debug]                             register additional parsers via
[debug]                             @register_model_config_parser.
[debug]       --json-model-override-args JSON_MODEL_OVERRIDE_ARGS
[debug]                             A dictionary in JSON string format used to override
[debug]                             default model configurations.
[debug]       --host HOST           The host of the HTTP server.
[debug]       --port PORT           The port of the HTTP server.
[debug]       --fastapi-root-path FASTAPI_ROOT_PATH
[debug]                             App is behind a path based routing proxy.
[debug]       --grpc-mode           If set, use gRPC server instead of HTTP server.
[debug]       --skip-server-warmup  If set, skip warmup.
[debug]       --warmups WARMUPS     Specify custom warmup functions (csv) to run before
[debug]                             server starts eg. --warmups=warmup_name1,warmup_name2
[debug]                             will run the functions `warmup_name1` and
[debug]                             `warmup_name2` specified in warmup.py before the
[debug]                             server starts listening for requests
[debug]       --enable-http2        Use Granian instead of Uvicorn as the ASGI server,
[debug]                             enabling HTTP/1.1 and HTTP/2 auto-negotiation. Clients
[debug]                             may use h2c (cleartext HTTP/2) or plain HTTP/1.1.
[debug]                             Requires 'pip install sglang[http2]'.
[debug]       --ssl-keyfile SSL_KEYFILE
[debug]                             The file path to the SSL key file.
[debug]       --ssl-certfile SSL_CERTFILE
[debug]                             The file path to the SSL certificate file.
[debug]       --ssl-ca-certs SSL_CA_CERTS
[debug]                             The CA certificates file.
[debug]       --ssl-keyfile-password SSL_KEYFILE_PASSWORD
[debug]                             The password to decrypt the SSL keyfile.
[debug]       --enable-ssl-refresh  Enable automatic SSL certificate hot-reloading when
[debug]                             cert/key files change on disk. Requires --ssl-certfile
[debug]                             and --ssl-keyfile.
[debug]       --dtype {auto,half,float16,bfloat16,float,float32}
[debug]                             Data type for model weights and activations. * "auto"
[debug]                             will use FP16 precision for FP32 and FP16 models, and
[debug]                             BF16 precision for BF16 models. * "half" for FP16.
[debug]                             Recommended for AWQ quantization. * "float16" is the
[debug]                             same as "half". * "bfloat16" for a balance between
[debug]                             precision and range. * "float" is shorthand for FP32
[debug]                             precision. * "float32" for FP32 precision.
[debug]       --quantization {awq,fp8,mxfp8,gptq,marlin,gptq_marlin,awq_marlin,bitsandbytes,gguf,modelopt,modelopt_fp8,modelopt_fp4,nvfp4_online,modelopt_mixed,petit_nvfp4,w8a8_int8,w8a8_fp8,moe_wna16,qoq,w4afp8,mxfp4,auto-round,compressed-tensors,modelslim,quark,quark_int4fp8_moe,quark_mxfp4,mlx_q4,mlx_q8,unquant}
[debug]                             The quantization method.
[debug]       --quantization-param-path QUANTIZATION_PARAM_PATH
[debug]                             Path to the JSON file containing the KV cache scaling
[debug]                             factors. This should generally be supplied, when KV
[debug]                             cache dtype is FP8. Otherwise, KV cache scaling
[debug]                             factors default to 1.0, which may cause accuracy
[debug]                             issues.
[debug]       --kv-cache-dtype {auto,fp8_e5m2,fp8_e4m3,bf16,bfloat16,fp4_e2m1}
[debug]                             Data type for kv cache storage. "auto" will use model
[debug]                             data type. "bf16" or "bfloat16" for BF16 KV cache.
[debug]                             "fp8_e5m2" and "fp8_e4m3" are supported for CUDA
[debug]                             11.8+. "fp4_e2m1" (only mxfp4) is supported for CUDA
[debug]                             12.8+ and PyTorch 2.8.0+
[debug]       --enable-fp32-lm-head
[debug]                             If set, the LM head outputs (logits) are in FP32.
[debug]       --modelopt-quant MODELOPT_QUANT
[debug]                             The ModelOpt quantization configuration. Supported
[debug]                             values: 'fp8', 'int4_awq', 'w4a8_awq', 'nvfp4',
[debug]                             'nvfp4_awq'. This requires the NVIDIA Model Optimizer
[debug]                             library to be installed: pip install nvidia-modelopt
[debug]       --modelopt-checkpoint-restore-path MODELOPT_CHECKPOINT_RESTORE_PATH
[debug]                             Path to restore a previously saved ModelOpt quantized
[debug]                             checkpoint. If provided, the quantization process will
[debug]                             be skipped and the model will be loaded from this
[debug]                             checkpoint.
[debug]       --modelopt-checkpoint-save-path MODELOPT_CHECKPOINT_SAVE_PATH
[debug]                             Path to save the ModelOpt quantized checkpoint after
[debug]                             quantization. This allows reusing the quantized model
[debug]                             in future runs.
[debug]       --modelopt-export-path MODELOPT_EXPORT_PATH
[debug]                             Path to export the quantized model in HuggingFace
[debug]                             format after ModelOpt quantization. The exported model
[debug]                             can then be used directly with SGLang for inference.
[debug]                             If not provided, the model will not be exported.
[debug]       --quantize-and-serve  Quantize the model with ModelOpt and immediately serve
[debug]                             it without exporting. This is useful for development
[debug]                             and prototyping. For production, it's recommended to
[debug]                             use separate quantization and deployment steps.
[debug]       --rl-quant-profile RL_QUANT_PROFILE
[debug]                             Path to the FlashRL quantization profile. Required
[debug]                             when using --load-format flash_rl.
[debug]       --mem-fraction-static MEM_FRACTION_STATIC
[debug]                             The fraction of the memory used for static allocation
[debug]                             (model weights and KV cache memory pool). Use a
[debug]                             smaller value if you see out-of-memory errors.
[debug]       --max-running-requests MAX_RUNNING_REQUESTS
[debug]                             The maximum number of running requests.
[debug]       --max-queued-requests MAX_QUEUED_REQUESTS
[debug]                             The maximum number of queued requests. This option is
[debug]                             ignored when using disaggregation-mode.
[debug]       --max-total-tokens MAX_TOTAL_TOKENS
[debug]                             The maximum number of tokens in the memory pool. If
[debug]                             not specified, it will be automatically calculated
[debug]                             based on the memory usage fraction. This option is
[debug]                             typically used for development and debugging purposes.
[debug]                             Supports standard SI suffixes (k, M, G, T) and IEC
[debug]                             suffixes (Ki, Mi, Gi, Ti). Suffixes are case-
[debug]                             sensitive. Decimals are allowed for SI suffixes only.
[debug]                             Examples: '1k' -> 1000 '1M' -> 1000000 '25.6k' ->
[debug]                             25600 '1Ki' -> 1024 '1Mi' -> 1048576
[debug]       --chunked-prefill-size CHUNKED_PREFILL_SIZE
[debug]                             The maximum number of tokens in a chunk for the
[debug]                             chunked prefill. Setting this to -1 means disabling
[debug]                             chunked prefill.
[debug]       --enable-dynamic-chunking
[debug]                             Enable dynamic chunk size adjustment for pipeline
[debug]                             parallelism. When enabled, chunk sizes are dynamically
[debug]                             calculated based on fitted function to maintain
[debug]                             consistent execution time across chunks.
[debug]       --max-prefill-tokens MAX_PREFILL_TOKENS
[debug]                             The maximum number of tokens in a prefill batch. The
[debug]                             real bound will be the maximum of this value and the
[debug]                             model's maximum context length. Supports standard SI
[debug]                             suffixes (k, M, G, T) and IEC suffixes (Ki, Mi, Gi,
[debug]                             Ti). Suffixes are case-sensitive. Decimals are allowed
[debug]                             for SI suffixes only. Examples: '1k' -> 1000 '1M' ->
[debug]                             1000000 '25.6k' -> 25600 '1Ki' -> 1024 '1Mi' ->
[debug]                             1048576
[debug]       --prefill-max-requests PREFILL_MAX_REQUESTS
[debug]                             The maximum number of requests in a prefill batch. If
[debug]                             not specified, there is no limit.
[debug]       --schedule-policy {lpm,random,fcfs,dfs-weight,lof,priority,routing-key}
[debug]                             The scheduling policy of the requests.
[debug]       --enable-priority-scheduling
[debug]                             Enable priority scheduling. Requests with higher
[debug]                             priority integer values will be scheduled first by
[debug]                             default.
[debug]       --disable-priority-preemption
[debug]                             Disable priority scheduling preemption.
[debug]       --default-priority-value DEFAULT_PRIORITY_VALUE
[debug]                             Default priority for requests without explicit
[debug]                             priority.
[debug]       --abort-on-priority-when-disabled
[debug]                             If set, abort requests that specify a priority when
[debug]                             priority scheduling is disabled.
[debug]       --schedule-low-priority-values-first
[debug]                             If specified with --enable-priority-scheduling, the
[debug]                             scheduler will schedule requests with lower priority
[debug]                             integer values first.
[debug]       --priority-scheduling-preemption-threshold PRIORITY_SCHEDULING_PREEMPTION_THRESHOLD
[debug]                             Minimum difference in priorities for an incoming
[debug]                             request to have to preempt running request(s).
[debug]       --schedule-conservativeness SCHEDULE_CONSERVATIVENESS
[debug]                             How conservative the schedule policy is. A larger
[debug]                             value means more conservative scheduling. Use a larger
[debug]                             value if you see requests being retracted frequently.
[debug]       --page-size PAGE_SIZE
[debug]                             The number of tokens in a page.
[debug]       --swa-full-tokens-ratio SWA_FULL_TOKENS_RATIO
[debug]                             The ratio of SWA layer KV tokens / full layer KV
[debug]                             tokens, regardless of the number of swa:full layers.
[debug]                             It should be between 0 and 1. E.g. 0.5 means if each
[debug]                             swa layer has 50 tokens, then each full layer has 100
[debug]                             tokens.
[debug]       --disable-hybrid-swa-memory
[debug]                             Disable the hybrid SWA memory pool.
[debug]       --radix-eviction-policy {lru,lfu,slru,priority}
[debug]                             The eviction policy of radix trees. 'lru' stands for
[debug]                             Least Recently Used, 'lfu' stands for Least Frequently
[debug]                             Used, 'slru' stands for Segmented Least Recently Used,
[debug]                             and 'priority' evicts lower-priority requests first.
[debug]       --prefill-only-disable-kv-cache
[debug]                             Skip the physical KV cache allocation for embedding-
[debug]                             mode prefill-only workloads. Currently only valid with
[debug]                             --is-embedding, --chunked-prefill-size=-1, --disable-
[debug]                             radix-cache, an FA prefill backend, and non-FP4 KV
[debug]                             cache so the fa_skip_kv_cache path is active (no layer
[debug]                             reads or writes the cache). Other prefill-only
[debug]                             workloads such as scoring/MIS may benefit from this
[debug]                             later once their attention paths stop using paged KV.
[debug]                             Scheduler admission accounting is unchanged; per-layer
[debug]                             K/V tensors are sized to (page_size, head_num,
[debug]                             head_dim) placeholders so GPU memory is not wasted.
[debug]       --disable-radix-cache
[debug]                             Disable RadixAttention for prefix caching.
[debug]       --disable-chunked-prefix-cache
[debug]                             Disable chunked prefix cache feature for deepseek,
[debug]                             which should save overhead for short sequences.
[debug]       --disable-overlap-schedule
[debug]                             Disable the overlap scheduler, which overlaps the CPU
[debug]                             scheduler with GPU model worker.
[debug]       --num-continuous-decode-steps NUM_CONTINUOUS_DECODE_STEPS
[debug]                             Run multiple continuous decoding steps to reduce
[debug]                             scheduling overhead. This can potentially increase
[debug]                             throughput but may also increase time-to-first-token
[debug]                             latency. The default value is 1, meaning only run one
[debug]                             decoding step at a time.
[debug]       --scheduler-recv-interval SCHEDULER_RECV_INTERVAL
[debug]                             The interval to poll requests in scheduler. Can be set
[debug]                             to >1 to reduce the overhead of this.
[debug]       --enable-mixed-chunk  Enabling mixing prefill and decode in a batch when
[debug]                             using chunked prefill.
[debug]       --device DEVICE       The device to use ('cuda', 'xpu', 'hpu', 'npu', 'cpu',
[debug]                             'musa'). Defaults to auto-detection if not specified.
[debug]       --base-gpu-id BASE_GPU_ID
[debug]                             The base GPU ID to start allocating GPUs from. Useful
[debug]                             when running multiple instances on the same machine.
[debug]       --gpu-id-step GPU_ID_STEP
[debug]                             The delta between consecutive GPU IDs that are used.
[debug]                             For example, setting it to 2 will use GPU 0,2,4,...
[debug]       --random-seed RANDOM_SEED
[debug]                             The random seed.
[debug]       --watchdog-timeout WATCHDOG_TIMEOUT
[debug]                             Set watchdog timeout in seconds. If a forward batch
[debug]                             takes longer than this, the server will crash to
[debug]                             prevent hanging.
[debug]       --soft-watchdog-timeout SOFT_WATCHDOG_TIMEOUT
[debug]                             Set soft watchdog timeout in seconds. If a forward
[debug]                             batch takes longer than this, the server will dump
[debug]                             information for debugging.
[debug]       --sleep-on-idle       Reduce CPU usage when sglang is idle.
[debug]       --use-ray             Use Ray actors for scheduler process management.
[debug]       --numa-node NUMA_NODE [NUMA_NODE ...]
[debug]                             Sets the numa node for the subprocesses. i-th element
[debug]                             corresponds to i-th subprocess. If unset, will be
[debug]                             automatically detected on NUMA systems.
[debug]       --gc-threshold GC_THRESHOLD [GC_THRESHOLD ...]
[debug]                             Set the garbage collection thresholds (the collection
[debug]                             frequency). Accepts 1 to 3 integers.
[debug]       --nccl-port NCCL_PORT
[debug]                             The port for NCCL distributed environment setup.
[debug]                             Defaults to a random port.
[debug]       --dist-timeout DIST_TIMEOUT
[debug]                             Set timeout for torch.distributed initialization.
[debug]       --dist-init-addr DIST_INIT_ADDR, --nccl-init-addr DIST_INIT_ADDR
[debug]                             The host address for initializing distributed backend
[debug]                             (e.g., `192.168.0.2:25000`).
[debug]       --nnodes NNODES       The number of nodes.
[debug]       --node-rank NODE_RANK
[debug]                             The node rank.
[debug]       --tp-size TP_SIZE, --tensor-parallel-size TP_SIZE
[debug]                             The tensor parallelism size.
[debug]       --pp-size PP_SIZE, --pipeline-parallel-size PP_SIZE
[debug]                             The pipeline parallelism size.
[debug]       --pp-max-micro-batch-size PP_MAX_MICRO_BATCH_SIZE
[debug]                             The maximum micro batch size in pipeline parallelism.
[debug]       --pp-async-batch-depth PP_ASYNC_BATCH_DEPTH
[debug]                             The async batch depth of pipeline parallelism.
[debug]       --dp-size DP_SIZE, --data-parallel-size DP_SIZE
[debug]                             The data parallelism size.
[debug]       --load-balance-method {auto,round_robin,follow_bootstrap_room,total_requests,total_tokens}
[debug]                             The load balancing strategy for data parallelism.
[debug]       --attn-cp-size ATTN_CP_SIZE, --attention-context-parallel-size ATTN_CP_SIZE
[debug]                             The attention context parallelism size.
[debug]       --moe-dp-size MOE_DP_SIZE, --moe-data-parallel-size MOE_DP_SIZE
[debug]                             The moe data parallelism size.
[debug]       --enable-prefill-cp   Enable context parallelism for the prefill phase.
[debug]                             Select the layout with --cp-strategy.
[debug]       --cp-strategy {zigzag,interleave}
[debug]                             Sharding strategy for prefill CP. 'zigzag' is the
[debug]                             former in-seq-split mode; 'interleave' is the former
[debug]                             round-robin-split mode.
[debug]       --enable-dp-attention
[debug]                             Enabling data parallelism for attention and tensor
[debug]                             parallelism for FFN. The dp size should be equal to
[debug]                             the tp size. Currently DeepSeek-V2 and Qwen 2/3 MoE
[debug]                             models are supported.
[debug]       --enable-dp-attention-local-control-broadcast
[debug]                             With DP-attention, send control messages to every DP
[debug]                             group leader and broadcast within attn_tp_group
[debug]                             instead of the full tp_group. Eliminates a costly all-
[debug]                             ranks gloo sync on every scheduler iteration.
[debug]       --enable-dp-lm-head   Enable vocabulary parallel across the attention TP
[debug]                             group to avoid all-gather across DP groups, optimizing
[debug]                             performance under DP attention.
[debug]       --enable-attn-tp-input-scattered
[debug]                             Allow input of attention to be scattered when only
[debug]                             using tensor parallelism, to reduce the computational
[debug]                             load of operations such as qkv latent.
[debug]       --disable-attn-tp-gather
[debug]                             Disable scheduler-side attn_tp_gather (the upstream SP
[debug]                             path that pads num_tokens to attn_tp_size and pre-
[debug]                             allocates a gathered buffer). Use for models that
[debug]                             manage SP scatter/gather at the model level (e.g.,
[debug]                             perform their own all_gather/reduce_scatter inside
[debug]                             attention) and do not consume the upstream
[debug]                             gathered_buffer. Without this, the cuda graph runner
[debug]                             pads num_tokens to attn_tp_size, which can cause
[debug]                             kernel autotuners to select wrong-sized variants at
[debug]                             small batches.
[debug]       --enable-p2p-check    Enable P2P check for GPU access, otherwise the p2p
[debug]                             access is allowed by default.
[debug]       --stream-interval STREAM_INTERVAL
[debug]                             The interval (or buffer size) for streaming in terms
[debug]                             of the token length. A smaller value makes streaming
[debug]                             smoother, while a larger value makes the throughput
[debug]                             higher
[debug]       --batch-notify-size BATCH_NOTIFY_SIZE
[debug]                             Number of streaming notifications to batch before
[debug]                             yielding to the event loop. Reduces asyncio wakeup
[debug]                             overhead under high concurrency.
[debug]       --stream-response-default-include-usage
[debug]                             Include usage in every streaming response (even when
[debug]                             stream_options is not specified).
[debug]       --incremental-streaming-output
[debug]                             Whether to output as a sequence of disjoint segments.
[debug]       --enable-streaming-session
[debug]                             Enable streaming session mode and StreamingSession
[debug]                             wrapper.
[debug]       --constrained-json-whitespace-pattern CONSTRAINED_JSON_WHITESPACE_PATTERN
[debug]                             (outlines and llguidance backends only) Regex pattern
[debug]                             for syntactic whitespaces allowed in JSON constrained
[debug]                             output. For example, to allow the model generate
[debug]                             consecutive whitespaces, set the pattern to [ ]*
[debug]       --constrained-json-disable-any-whitespace
[debug]                             (xgrammar and llguidance backends only) Enforce
[debug]                             compact representation in JSON constrained output.
[debug]       --log-level LOG_LEVEL
[debug]                             The logging level of all loggers.
[debug]       --log-level-http LOG_LEVEL_HTTP
[debug]                             The logging level of HTTP server. If not set, reuse
[debug]                             --log-level by default.
[debug]       --log-requests        Log metadata, inputs, outputs of all requests. The
[debug]                             verbosity is decided by --log-requests-level
[debug]       --log-requests-level {0,1,2,3}
[debug]                             0: Log metadata (no sampling parameters). 1: Log
[debug]                             metadata and sampling parameters. 2: Log metadata,
[debug]                             sampling parameters and partial input/output. 3: Log
[debug]                             every input/output.
[debug]       --log-requests-format {text,json}
[debug]                             Format for request logging: 'text' (human-readable) or
[debug]                             'json' (structured)
[debug]       --log-requests-target LOG_REQUESTS_TARGET [LOG_REQUESTS_TARGET ...]
[debug]                             Target(s) for request logging: 'stdout' and/or
[debug]                             directory path(s) for file output. Can specify
[debug]                             multiple targets, e.g., '--log-requests-target stdout
[debug]                             /my/path'.
[debug]       --uvicorn-access-log-exclude-prefixes [UVICORN_ACCESS_LOG_EXCLUDE_PREFIXES ...]
[debug]                             Exclude uvicorn access logs whose request path starts
[debug]                             with any of these prefixes. Defaults to empty
[debug]                             (disabled). Example: --uvicorn-access-log-exclude-
[debug]                             prefixes /metrics /health
[debug]       --crash-dump-folder CRASH_DUMP_FOLDER
[debug]                             Folder path to dump requests from the last 5 min
[debug]                             before a crash (if any). If not specified, crash
[debug]                             dumping is disabled.
[debug]       --show-time-cost      Show time cost of custom marks.
[debug]       --enable-metrics      Enable log prometheus metrics.
[debug]       --grpc-http-sidecar-port GRPC_HTTP_SIDECAR_PORT
[debug]                             Port for the HTTP sidecar server in gRPC mode (--grpc-
[debug]                             mode). Serves Prometheus metrics and profiling
[debug]                             endpoints. Defaults to --port + 1. Not used in HTTP
[debug]                             mode.
[debug]       --enable-mfu-metrics  Enable estimated MFU-related prometheus metrics.
[debug]       --enable-metrics-for-all-schedulers
[debug]                             Enable --enable-metrics-for-all-schedulers when you
[debug]                             want schedulers on all TP ranks (not just TP 0) to
[debug]                             record request metrics separately. This is especially
[debug]                             useful when dp_attention is enabled, as otherwise all
[debug]                             metrics appear to come from TP 0.
[debug]       --load-snapshot-publish-interval LOAD_SNAPSHOT_PUBLISH_INTERVAL
[debug]                             Publish load snapshot to shared memory every N decode
[debug]                             iterations. Prefill and idle always publish
[debug]                             immediately.
[debug]       --tokenizer-metrics-custom-labels-header TOKENIZER_METRICS_CUSTOM_LABELS_HEADER
[debug]                             Specify the HTTP header for passing custom labels for
[debug]                             tokenizer metrics.
[debug]       --tokenizer-metrics-allowed-custom-labels TOKENIZER_METRICS_ALLOWED_CUSTOM_LABELS [TOKENIZER_METRICS_ALLOWED_CUSTOM_LABELS ...]
[debug]                             The custom labels allowed for tokenizer metrics. The
[debug]                             labels are specified via a dict in '--tokenizer-
[debug]                             metrics-custom-labels-header' field in HTTP requests,
[debug]                             e.g., {'label1': 'value1', 'label2': 'value2'} is
[debug]                             allowed if '--tokenizer-metrics-allowed-custom-labels
[debug]                             label1 label2' is set.
[debug]       --extra-metric-labels EXTRA_METRIC_LABELS
[debug]                             The custom labels for metrics. e.g. '{"label1":
[debug]                             "value1", "label2": "value2"}'
[debug]       --bucket-time-to-first-token BUCKET_TIME_TO_FIRST_TOKEN [BUCKET_TIME_TO_FIRST_TOKEN ...]
[debug]                             The buckets of time to first token, specified as a
[debug]                             list of floats.
[debug]       --bucket-inter-token-latency BUCKET_INTER_TOKEN_LATENCY [BUCKET_INTER_TOKEN_LATENCY ...]
[debug]                             The buckets of inter-token latency, specified as a
[debug]                             list of floats.
[debug]       --bucket-e2e-request-latency BUCKET_E2E_REQUEST_LATENCY [BUCKET_E2E_REQUEST_LATENCY ...]
[debug]                             The buckets of end-to-end request latency, specified
[debug]                             as a list of floats.
[debug]       --prompt-tokens-buckets PROMPT_TOKENS_BUCKETS [PROMPT_TOKENS_BUCKETS ...]
[debug]                             The buckets rule of prompt tokens. Supports 3 rule
[debug]                             types: 'default' uses predefined buckets; 'tse
[debug]                             <middle> <base> <count>' generates two sides
[debug]                             exponential distributed buckets (e.g., 'tse 1000 2 8'
[debug]                             generates buckets [984.0, 992.0, 996.0, 998.0, 1000.0,
[debug]                             1002.0, 1004.0, 1008.0, 1016.0]).); 'custom <value1>
[debug]                             <value2> ...' uses custom bucket values (e.g., 'custom
[debug]                             10 50 100 500').
[debug]       --generation-tokens-buckets GENERATION_TOKENS_BUCKETS [GENERATION_TOKENS_BUCKETS ...]
[debug]                             The buckets rule for generation tokens histogram.
[debug]                             Supports 3 rule types: 'default' uses predefined
[debug]                             buckets; 'tse <middle> <base> <count>' generates two
[debug]                             sides exponential distributed buckets (e.g., 'tse 1000
[debug]                             2 8' generates buckets [984.0, 992.0, 996.0, 998.0,
[debug]                             1000.0, 1002.0, 1004.0, 1008.0, 1016.0]).); 'custom
[debug]                             <value1> <value2> ...' uses custom bucket values
[debug]                             (e.g., 'custom 10 50 100 500').
[debug]       --gc-warning-threshold-secs GC_WARNING_THRESHOLD_SECS
[debug]                             The threshold for long GC warning. If a GC takes
[debug]                             longer than this, a warning will be logged. Set to 0
[debug]                             to disable.
[debug]       --decode-log-interval DECODE_LOG_INTERVAL
[debug]                             The log and metrics reporting interval (in decode
[debug]                             iterations) for decode batches.
[debug]       --enable-request-time-stats-logging
[debug]                             Enable per request time stats logging
[debug]       --kv-events-config KV_EVENTS_CONFIG
[debug]                             Config in json format for NVIDIA dynamo KV event
[debug]                             publishing. Publishing will be enabled if this flag is
[debug]                             used.
[debug]       --enable-forward-pass-metrics
[debug]                             Enable per-iteration forward pass metrics via ZMQ IPC.
[debug]                             External consumers (e.g. Dynamo planner) subscribe to
[debug]                             the IPC endpoint exposed in
[debug]                             server_args.forward_pass_metrics_ipc_name.
[debug]       --enable-trace        Enable opentelemetry trace

[debug]       --export-metrics-to-file

[debug]       --chat-template CHAT_TEMPLATE

[debug]                             compatible API server.
[debug]       --hf-chat-template-name HF_CHAT_TEMPLATE_NAME
[debug]                             When the HuggingFace tokenizer has multiple chat
[debug]                             templates (e.g., 'default', 'tool_use', 'rag'),
[debug]                             specify which named template to use. If not set, the
[debug]                             first available template is used.
[debug]       --completion-template COMPLETION_TEMPLATE
[debug]                             The buliltin completion template name or the path of
[debug]                             the completion template file. This is only used for
[debug]                             OpenAI-compatible API server. only for code completion
[debug]                             currently.
[debug]       --file-storage-path FILE_STORAGE_PATH
[debug]                             The path of the file storage in backend.
[debug]       --enable-cache-report
[debug]                             Return number of cached tokens in
[debug]                             usage.prompt_tokens_details for each openai request.
[debug]       --strip-thinking-cache
[debug]                             Skip caching reasoning-model output (thinking +
[debug]                             answer) in the radix tree on finish; keep only the
[debug]                             prompt prefix. Opt-in: changes cache contents.
[debug]       --enable-strict-thinking
[debug]                             Enable strict token filtering during the thinking
[debug]                             phase. Blocks model-specific excluded tokens (e.g.,
[debug]                             tool call markers) during reasoning. Requires a
[debug]                             grammar backend that supports token filtering.
[debug]       --tool-server TOOL_SERVER
[debug]                             Either 'demo' or a comma-separated list of tool server
[debug]                             urls to use for the model. If not specified, no tool
[debug]                             server will be used.
[debug]       --sampling-defaults {openai,model}
[debug]                             Where to get default sampling parameters. 'openai'
[debug]                             uses SGLang/OpenAI defaults (temperature=1.0,
[debug]                             top_p=1.0, etc.). 'model' uses the model's
[debug]                             generation_config.json to get the recommended sampling
[debug]                             parameters if available. Default is 'model'.
[debug]       --asr-max-buffer-seconds ASR_MAX_BUFFER_SECONDS
[debug]                             Maximum seconds of PCM audio the streaming ASR
[debug]                             WebSocket handler will accumulate before closing the
[debug]                             session with a buffer_overflow error. Guards against
[debug]                             OOM when a client streams audio faster than inference
[debug]                             can consume it. Default 60s.
[debug]       --asr-max-concurrent-sessions ASR_MAX_CONCURRENT_SESSIONS
[debug]                             Maximum number of concurrent realtime ASR WebSocket
[debug]                             sessions served by /v1/realtime. New connections
[debug]                             beyond this cap are accepted, sent an
[debug]                             error{code:too_many_sessions} frame, and closed.
[debug]                             Default 32.
[debug]       --preferred-sampling-params PREFERRED_SAMPLING_PARAMS
[debug]                             json-formatted sampling settings that will be returned
[debug]                             in /get_model_info
[debug]       --allow-auto-truncate
[debug]                             Allow automatically truncating requests that exceed
[debug]                             the maximum input length instead of returning an
[debug]                             error.
[debug]       --enable-prefill-delayer
[debug]                             Enable prefill delayer for DP attention to reduce idle
[debug]                             time.
[debug]       --prefill-delayer-max-delay-passes PREFILL_DELAYER_MAX_DELAY_PASSES
[debug]                             Maximum forward passes to delay prefill.
[debug]       --prefill-delayer-token-usage-low-watermark PREFILL_DELAYER_TOKEN_USAGE_LOW_WATERMARK
[debug]                             Token usage low watermark for prefill delayer.
[debug]       --prefill-delayer-forward-passes-buckets PREFILL_DELAYER_FORWARD_PASSES_BUCKETS [PREFILL_DELAYER_FORWARD_PASSES_BUCKETS ...]
[debug]                             Custom buckets for prefill delayer forward passes
[debug]                             histogram. 0 and max_delay_passes-1 will be auto-
[debug]                             added.
[debug]       --prefill-delayer-wait-seconds-buckets PREFILL_DELAYER_WAIT_SECONDS_BUCKETS [PREFILL_DELAYER_WAIT_SECONDS_BUCKETS ...]
[debug]                             Custom buckets for prefill delayer wait seconds
[debug]                             histogram. 0 will be auto-added.
[debug]       --prefill-delayer-queue-min-ratio PREFILL_DELAYER_QUEUE_MIN_RATIO
[debug]                             Opt-in to the adaptive queue-based delay trigger
[debug]                             (independent of the slot-based one). Delays prefill
[debug]                             until the waiting queue reaches min(running_req *
[debug]                             ratio, max_prefill_bs) so small fragments batch into a
[debug]                             larger prefill. Unset (default) keeps the original
[debug]                             slot-only behavior. Typical: 0.1 ~ 0.5.
[debug]       --prefill-delayer-max-delay-ms PREFILL_DELAYER_MAX_DELAY_MS
[debug]                             Wall-clock cap (ms) on a single queue-trigger delay;
[debug]                             once exceeded, prefill is force-released to bound
[debug]                             worst-case TTFT. Only consulted when --prefill-
[debug]                             delayer-queue-min-ratio is set. Typical: 1000 ~ 5000;
[debug]                             defaults to 5000 if unset.
[debug]       --enable-lora         Enable LoRA support for the model. This argument is
[debug]                             automatically set to True if `--lora-paths` is
[debug]                             provided for backward compatibility.
[debug]       --enable-lora-overlap-loading
[debug]                             Enable asynchronous LoRA weight loading in order to
[debug]                             overlap H2D transfers with GPU compute. This should be
[debug]                             enabled if you find that your LoRA workloads are
[debug]                             bottlenecked by adapter weight loading, for example
[debug]                             when frequently loading large LoRA adapters.
[debug]       --max-lora-rank MAX_LORA_RANK
[debug]                             The maximum rank of LoRA adapters. If not specified,
[debug]                             it will be automatically inferred from the adapters
[debug]                             provided in --lora-paths.
[debug]       --lora-target-modules [{q_proj,k_proj,v_proj,o_proj,q_a_proj,kv_a_proj_with_mqa,q_b_proj,kv_b_proj,wq_b,wk,weights_proj,gate_proj,up_proj,down_proj,qkv_proj,gate_up_proj,embed_tokens,lm_head,all} ...]
[debug]                             The union set of all target modules where LoRA should
[debug]                             be applied. If not specified, it will be automatically
[debug]                             inferred from the adapters provided in --lora-paths.
[debug]                             If 'all' is specified, all supported modules will be
[debug]                             targeted.
[debug]       --lora-paths [LORA_PATHS ...]
[debug]                             The list of LoRA adapters to load. Each adapter must
[debug]                             be specified in one of the following formats: <PATH> |
[debug]                             <NAME>=<PATH> | JSON with schema
[debug]                             {"lora_name":str,"lora_path":str,"pinned":bool}
[debug]       --max-loaded-loras MAX_LOADED_LORAS
[debug]                             If specified, it limits the maximum number of LoRA
[debug]                             adapters loaded in CPU memory at a time. The value
[debug]                             must be greater than or equal to `--max-loras-per-
[debug]                             batch`.
[debug]       --max-loras-per-batch MAX_LORAS_PER_BATCH
[debug]                             Maximum number of adapters for a running batch,
[debug]                             include base-only request.
[debug]       --lora-eviction-policy {lru,fifo}
[debug]                             LoRA adapter eviction policy when memory pool is full.
[debug]                             'lru': Least Recently Used (default, better cache
[debug]                             efficiency). 'fifo': First-In-First-Out.
[debug]       --lora-backend {triton,csgmv,ascend,torch_native}
[debug]                             Choose the kernel backend for multi-LoRA serving.
[debug]       --max-lora-chunk-size {16,32,64,128}
[debug]                             Maximum chunk size for the ChunkedSGMV LoRA backend.
[debug]                             Only used when --lora-backend is 'csgmv'. Choosing a
[debug]                             larger value might improve performance.
[debug]       --experts-shared-outer-loras, --no-experts-shared-outer-loras
[debug]                             Force shared outer LoRA mode for MoE models. When set,
[debug]                             w1/w3 lora_A and w2 lora_B are shared across experts
[debug]                             (expert_dim=1). Use --no-experts-shared-outer-loras to
[debug]                             force disable. By default this is auto-detected from
[debug]                             adapter weights.
[debug]       --lora-use-virtual-experts
[debug]                             Enable virtual expert computation for MoE models. When
[debug]                             set, the model will use virtual expert computation.
[debug]       --lora-strict-loading, --no-lora-strict-loading
[debug]                             Enable strict loading for LoRA adapters. When set,
[debug]                             mismatched or missing keys in the adapter weights will
[debug]                             raise an error.
[debug]       --lora-drain-wait-threshold LORA_DRAIN_WAIT_THRESHOLD
[debug]                             When any LoRA adapter request waits longer than this
[debug]                             threshold (in seconds), the scheduler will selectively
[debug]                             drain one running adapter to make room. This mitigates
[debug]                             extreme tail latency under high or skewed workloads by
[debug]                             preventing a small set of adapters from monopolizing
[debug]                             batch slots. Set to 0 to disable draining (default).
[debug]       --attention-backend {triton,torch_native,flex_attention,dsa,nsa,dsv4,compressed,cutlass_mla,fa3,fa4,flashinfer,flashmla,trtllm_mla,cutedsl_mla,tokenspeed_mla,trtllm_mha,dual_chunk_flash_attn,aiter,wave,intel_amx,ascend,intel_xpu}
[debug]                             Choose the kernels for attention layers.
[debug]       --decode-attention-backend {triton,torch_native,flex_attention,dsa,nsa,dsv4,compressed,cutlass_mla,fa3,fa4,flashinfer,flashmla,trtllm_mla,cutedsl_mla,tokenspeed_mla,trtllm_mha,dual_chunk_flash_attn,aiter,wave,intel_amx,ascend,intel_xpu}
[debug]                             Choose the kernels for decode attention layers (have
[debug]                             priority over --attention-backend).
[debug]       --prefill-attention-backend {triton,torch_native,flex_attention,dsa,nsa,dsv4,compressed,cutlass_mla,fa3,fa4,flashinfer,flashmla,trtllm_mla,cutedsl_mla,tokenspeed_mla,trtllm_mha,dual_chunk_flash_attn,aiter,wave,intel_amx,ascend,intel_xpu}
[debug]                             Choose the kernels for prefill attention layers (have
[debug]                             priority over --attention-backend).

[debug]       --radix-cache-backend RADIX_CACHE_BACKEND
[debug]                             Name of a radix-cache backend previously registered
[debug]                             via register_radix_cache_backend. Omit this flag to
[debug]                             use the built-in default cache selection chain.
[debug]       --mm-attention-backend {sdpa,fa3,fa4,triton_attn,ascend_attn,aiter_attn,flashinfer_cudnn,amx_attn,xpu_attn}
[debug]                             Set multimodal attention backend.
[debug]       --fp8-gemm-backend {auto,deep_gemm,flashinfer_trtllm,flashinfer_cutlass,flashinfer_deepgemm,cutlass,triton,aiter}
[debug]                             Choose the runner backend for Blockwise FP8 GEMM
[debug]                             operations. Options: 'auto' (default, auto-selects
[debug]                             based on hardware), 'deep_gemm' (JIT-compiled; enabled
[debug]                             by default on NVIDIA Hopper (SM90) and Blackwell
[debug]                             (SM100) when DeepGEMM is installed),
[debug]                             'flashinfer_trtllm' (optimal for Blackwell and low-
[debug]                             latency), 'flashinfer_cutlass' (FlashInfer CUTLASS
[debug]                             groupwise FP8 GEMM), 'flashinfer_deepgemm' (Hopper
[debug]                             SM90 only; uses swapAB optimization for small M
[debug]                             dimensions in decoding), 'cutlass' (optimal for
[debug]                             Hopper/Blackwell GPUs and high-throughput), 'triton'
[debug]                             (fallback, widely compatible), 'aiter' (ROCm only).
[debug]       --fp4-gemm-backend {auto,cutlass,flashinfer_cudnn,flashinfer_cutedsl,flashinfer_cutlass,flashinfer_trtllm,marlin}
[debug]                             Choose the runner backend for NVFP4 GEMM operations.
[debug]                             Options: 'auto' (default; selects flashinfer_cutedsl
[debug]                             on SM100, marlin on SM80-SM90, flashinfer_cutlass
[debug]                             otherwise (including SM120)), 'cutlass' (SGLang
[debug]                             CUTLASS kernel), 'flashinfer_cutlass' (FlashInfer
[debug]                             CUTLASS backend), 'flashinfer_cudnn' (FlashInfer cuDNN
[debug]                             backend, optimal on CUDA 13+ with cuDNN 9.15+),
[debug]                             'flashinfer_cutedsl' (FlashInfer CuTe DSL backend),
[debug]                             'flashinfer_trtllm' (FlashInfer TensorRT-LLM backend,
[debug]                             requires different weight preparation with shuffling),
[debug]                             'marlin' (weight-only W4A16 fallback for SM80+).
[debug]       --dsa-prefill-backend {flashmla_sparse,flashmla_kv,flashmla_auto,fa3,tilelang,aiter,trtllm}
[debug]                             DSA (DeepSeek Sparse Attention) prefill backend. If
[debug]                             not specified, auto-detects based on hardware and
[debug]                             kv_cache_dtype.
[debug]       --dsa-decode-backend {flashmla_sparse,flashmla_kv,flashmla_auto,fa3,tilelang,aiter,trtllm}

[debug]                             The accept probability of a draft token is raised from

[debug]                             Use rejection sampling for speculative decoding
[debug]                             (requires topk=1).

[debug]                             The path of the draft model's small vocab table.
[debug]       --speculative-attention-mode {prefill,decode}
[debug]                             Attention backend for speculative decoding operations
[debug]                             (both target verify and draft extend). Can be one of
[debug]                             'prefill' (default) or 'decode'.
[debug]       --speculative-draft-attention-backend SPECULATIVE_DRAFT_ATTENTION_BACKEND
[debug]                             Attention backend for speculative decoding drafting.
[debug]       --speculative-draft-window-size SPECULATIVE_DRAFT_WINDOW_SIZE
[debug]                             Sliding window size for the draft model. Honored by
[debug]                             Llama EAGLE-3 (`LlamaForCausalLMEagle3`) and DFLASH
[debug]                             only; other EAGLE-3 backends (e.g. MLA-based drafters)
[debug]                             silently ignore it. For Llama EAGLE-3, the drafter
[debug]                             only attends to the most recent N keys (verifier
[debug]                             hidden states + its own outputs); the verifier is
[debug]                             unaffected. For DFLASH, the draft worker keeps a
[debug]                             recent target-token window in its local KV cache
[debug]                             (paged backends may retain up to one extra page on the
[debug]                             left for alignment). Default is full
[debug]                             attention/context.
[debug]       --speculative-moe-runner-backend {auto,deep_gemm,triton,triton_kernel,flashinfer_trtllm,experimental_sgl_trtllm,flashinfer_trtllm_routed,flashinfer_cutlass,flashinfer_mxfp4,flashinfer_cutedsl,cutlass,aiter,marlin}

[debug]                             Enable DeepEP Waterfill: dispatch the shared expert as

[debug]                             Automatically sets --moe-a2a-backend deepep,
[debug]                             implicitly enables shared-expert fusion, and supports

[debug]       --linear-attn-decode-backend {triton,cutedsl,flashinfer}
[debug]                             Override the kernel backend for linear attention
[debug]                             decode. If not set, uses --linear-attn-backend.
[debug]       --linear-attn-prefill-backend {triton,cutedsl,flashinfer}
[debug]                             Override the kernel backend for linear attention
[debug]                             prefill/extend. If not set, uses --linear-attn-
[debug]                             backend.
[debug]       --enable-hierarchical-cache
[debug]                             Enable hierarchical cache
[debug]       --hicache-ratio HICACHE_RATIO
[debug]                             The ratio of the size of host KV cache memory pool to
[debug]                             the size of device pool.
[debug]       --hicache-size HICACHE_SIZE
[debug]                             The size of host KV cache memory pool in gigabytes,
[debug]                             which will override the hicache_ratio if set.
[debug]       --hicache-write-policy {write_back,write_through,write_through_selective}
[debug]                             The write policy of hierarchical cache.
[debug]       --hicache-io-backend {direct,kernel,kernel_ascend}
[debug]                             The IO backend for KV cache transfer between CPU and
[debug]                             GPU
[debug]       --hicache-mem-layout {layer_first,page_first,page_first_direct,page_first_kv_split,page_head}
[debug]                             The layout of host memory pool for hierarchical cache.
[debug]       --hicache-storage-backend {file,mooncake,hf3fs,nixl,aibrix,dynamic,eic,simm}
[debug]                             The storage backend for hierarchical KV cache. Built-
[debug]                             in backends: file, mooncake, hf3fs, nixl, aibrix. For
[debug]                             dynamic backend, use --hicache-storage-backend-extra-
[debug]                             config to specify: backend_name (custom name),
[debug]                             module_path (Python module path), class_name (backend
[debug]                             class name).
[debug]       --hicache-storage-prefetch-policy {best_effort,wait_complete,timeout}
[debug]                             Control when prefetching from the storage backend
[debug]                             should stop.
[debug]       --hicache-storage-backend-extra-config HICACHE_STORAGE_BACKEND_EXTRA_CONFIG
[debug]                             A dictionary in JSON string format, or a string
[debug]                             starting with a leading '@' and a config file in
[debug]                             JSON/YAML/TOML format, containing extra configuration
[debug]                             for the storage backend.
[debug]       --enable-hisparse     Enable hierarchical sparse attention
[debug]       --hisparse-config HISPARSE_CONFIG, --hierarchical-sparse-attention-extra-config HISPARSE_CONFIG
