[debug]       --cuda-graph-config CUDA_GRAPH_CONFIG
[debug]                             Per-phase CUDA graph settings as JSON, e.g. '{"decode"
[debug]                             :{"backend":"full","max_bs":256},"prefill":{"backend":
[debug]                             "tc_piecewise","tc_compiler":"eager"}}'. Allowed
[debug]                             backends per phase: full, breakable, tc_piecewise,
[debug]                             disabled (full is decode-only). JSON wins over the
[debug]                             per-phase --cuda-graph-* convenience flags and over
[debug]                             legacy flags.
[debug]       --cuda-graph-backend-decode {full,breakable,tc_piecewise,disabled}

[debug]                             cuda_graph_config[decode].backend.

[debug]       --cuda-graph-max-bs-decode CUDA_GRAPH_MAX_BS_DECODE

[debug]       --cuda-graph-max-bs-prefill CUDA_GRAPH_MAX_BS_PREFILL

[debug]                             graph.
[debug]       --cuda-graph-bs-decode CUDA_GRAPH_BS_DECODE [CUDA_GRAPH_BS_DECODE ...]

[debug]                             cuda graph.
[debug]       --cuda-graph-bs-prefill CUDA_GRAPH_BS_PREFILL [CUDA_GRAPH_BS_PREFILL ...]
[debug]                             Explicit list of batch sizes to capture for the
[debug]                             prefill cuda graph.
[debug]       --cuda-graph-tc-compiler {eager,inductor}

[debug]                             only the prefill phase consumes it).
[debug]       --disable-prefill-cuda-graph
[debug]                             Disable the prefill-phase CUDA graph. Convenience for

[debug]       --disable-decode-cuda-graph
[debug]                             Disable the decode-phase CUDA graph. Convenience for
[debug]                             --cuda-graph-backend-decode=disabled.
[debug]       --disable-cuda-graph-padding
[debug]                             Disable cuda graph when padding is needed. Still uses
[debug]                             cuda graph when padding is not needed.
[debug]       --enable-profile-cuda-graph
[debug]                             Enable profiling of cuda graph capture.
[debug]       --enable-cudagraph-gc
[debug]                             Enable garbage collection during CUDA graph capture.
[debug]                             If disabled (default), GC is frozen during capture to
[debug]                             speed up the process.
[debug]       --debug-cuda-graph    Enable debug/eager mode for CUDA graph using breakable
[debug]                             CUDA graph. When enabled, graph breaks are inserted so
[debug]                             every operation runs eagerly while still going through
[debug]                             the CUDA graph capture / replay path. Useful for
[debug]                             debugging CUDA graph capture / replay issues.
[debug]       --enable-layerwise-nvtx-marker
[debug]                             Enable layerwise NVTX profiling annotations for the

[debug]       --enable-nccl-nvls    Enable NCCL NVLS for prefill heavy requests when
[debug]                             available.
[debug]       --enable-symm-mem     Enable NCCL symmetric memory for fast collectives.
[debug]       --triton-attention-reduce-in-fp32
[debug]                             Cast the intermediate attention results to fp32 to
[debug]                             avoid possible crashes related to fp16.This only
[debug]                             affects Triton attention kernels.
[debug]       --triton-attention-num-kv-splits TRITON_ATTENTION_NUM_KV_SPLITS
[debug]                             The number of KV splits in flash decoding Triton
[debug]                             kernel. Larger value is better in longer context
[debug]                             scenarios. The default value is 8.
[debug]       --triton-attention-split-tile-size TRITON_ATTENTION_SPLIT_TILE_SIZE
[debug]                             The size of split KV tile in flash decoding Triton
[debug]                             kernel. Used for deterministic inference.

[debug]                             Enable corner alignment for resize of embeddings grid
[debug]                             to ensure more accurate(but slower) evaluation of
[debug]                             interpolated embedding values.
[debug]       --enable-fused-moe-sum-all-reduce
[debug]                             Enable fused moe triton and sum all reduce.

[debug]       --disable-custom-all-reduce
[debug]                             Disable the custom all-reduce kernel and fall back to

[debug]       --enable-mscclpp      Enable using mscclpp for small messages for all-reduce
[debug]                             kernel and fall back to NCCL.
[debug]       --enable-torch-symm-mem
[debug]                             Enable using torch symm mem for all-reduce kernel and
[debug]                             fall back to NCCL. Only supports CUDA device SM90 and
[debug]                             above. SM90 supports world size 4, 6, 8. SM100
[debug]                             supports world size 6, 8.
[debug]       --pre-warm-nccl       Pre-warm NCCL/RCCL communicators during startup to
[debug]                             reduce P99 TTFT cold-start latency. Default: enabled
[debug]                             for AMD/HIP (RCCL), disabled for NVIDIA/CUDA (NCCL).
[debug]       --enable-quant-communications
[debug]                             Enable INT8 quantization of TP communications (limited
[debug]                             support).
[debug]       --enforce-disable-flashinfer-allreduce-fusion
[debug]                             Enforce disable FlashInfer allreduce fusion.
[debug]       --flashinfer-allreduce-fusion-backend {auto,trtllm,mnnvl}
[debug]                             Enable FlashInfer allreduce fusion and choose backend.
[debug]                             Requires SM90 or SM10X NVIDIA GPUs. Defaults to auto.
[debug]                             'auto': choose mnnvl on Blackwell (SM100/SM103)
[debug]                             systems (single- and multi-node) and trtllm on SM90
[debug]                             single-node systems. 'trtllm': available on single-
[debug]                             node systems only. 'mnnvl': available on SM90 single-
[debug]                             node systems and SM100/SM103 single-node or multi-node
[debug]                             systems via MNNVL fabric. Fuses allreduce with
[debug]                             Residual + RMSNorm for supported MoE models.
